[Code Health] Modernize Java patterns across components/browser_ui

Modernizes legacy instanceof checks to Java 16/17+ pattern matching,
replaces string concatenations in production logs with format
specifiers, and simplifies string emptiness checks across
components/browser_ui.

Key changes:
- widget: Modernizes 11 instanceof checks with pattern matching in
  RadioButtonWithDescription, ContainmentViewStyler,
  AutoAnimatorDrawable, and DateDividedAdapter.
- widget: Converts Log.e and Log.w concatenations to format specifiers
  in DateDividedAdapter and RoundedIconGenerator.
- settings: Modernizes 6 instanceof checks with pattern matching in
  ChromeExpandableSwitchPreference and SettingsIndexData.
- settings: Simplifies query.equals("") to query.isEmpty() in
  SearchUtils.
- contacts_picker: Simplifies query.equals("") to query.isEmpty() in
  PickerAdapter.
- photo_picker: Modernizes instanceof in PickerAdapter and converts
  Log.i concatenations to format specifiers in FileEnumWorkerTask.
- bottomsheet: Modernizes instanceof in BottomSheetListViewBase.
- site_settings: Modernizes instanceof in WebsiteAddress#equals.
- util: Modernizes instanceof in ToolbarUtils.
- notifications: Modernizes instanceof in
  BaseNotificationManagerProxyFactory.

b5fbbfa53723e0c3a3401d55f58a796d1baa43b8 [ diff ] 
chrome: Fix app menu item activating wrong window on window change

App menu command ids indexed the live window list, so adding or removing
a window while the menu was open made items preview or activate the
wrong window. Cache the window list when building the menu, and null out
removed windows.

TAG=agy
CONV=4c84380b-b63f-4be0-878c-351d707c24f6

Revert CHECK in PreconnectManagerImpl::LookupProxyForUrl to DCHECK

The CHECK verifying that url.SchemeIsHTTPOrHTTPS() was triggered in
release builds when a non-HTTP/HTTPS URL was passed. This change reverts
the CHECK back to a DCHECK and adds the CHECK-exclusion comment.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/e47cfb7108400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

DownloadTargetDeterminer::RequestConfirmationDone

[histogram-cleanup] Remove Net.CertVerifier.AndroidTrustStoreInit

This removes the expired histogram
Net.CertVerifier.AndroidTrustStoreInit (expired 2024-04-28), which timed
how long it took to initialize the Android platform trust store in the
builtin certificate verifier. It expired long ago and nothing needs the
data anymore, so the timer is just dead code and stale metadata. I
dropped the scoped timer in
TrustStoreAndroid::MaybeInitializeAndGetImpl(), the histogram_macros
include that is no longer needed there, and the histogram entry in the
net histograms XML.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[Net.CertVerifier.AndroidTrustStoreInit]=expired

This change was generated using the skill histogram-cleanup.

Revert CHECK to DCHECK when virtual_path_ has .crdownload suffix

DoDetermineIntermediatePath contains a check ensuring virtual_path_ does
not end with .crdownload. A recently added CHECK macro caused crashes
when this condition was met. This change converts the CHECK back to a
DCHECK until further investigation can be done.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/a672ff6f2f800000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

tree
0babd804a74a04b986b0366502fadedee74f28ef
parent
821e18d4add56df35387fe67113b888706dd3853 [ diff ] 
Reland "[PiP] Unify PiP eligibility checks on Android"

This is a reland of commit 3be277cafff63ae2ef13e1d2290a59bf07f43a48

The original change was reverted because routing
FullscreenVideoPictureInPictureController#canDoPictureInPicture
through PictureInPicture#isEnabled also applied its Android R
requirement, which exists to avoid a web Picture-in-Picture crash
(b/143784148). That disabled fullscreen video PiP on Android 10, which
broke FullscreenVideoPictureInPictureControllerTest and
FullscreenVideoTest on the Android 10 bots.

Changes since the original change:
- Move the Android R check out of PictureInPicture#isEnabled into
  ActivityTabWebContentsDelegateAndroid#isPictureInPictureEnabled, the
  web Picture-in-Picture gate that it protects. Fullscreen video PiP
  keeps working on Android 10 while sharing all other checks, and the
  TODO there tracks removing the check once the minimum SDK is R.
- Stop pinning the fullscreen PiP unit tests to Android R, and add
  Android Q unit tests verifying that fullscreen video PiP is allowed
  and that web Picture-in-Picture stays disabled.
- Add the Android R check to the web Picture-in-Picture test oracles
  in ContextMenuTest and TabAlertIndicatorTest. They relied on
  PictureInPicture#isEnabled and would otherwise expect web PiP on
  Android 10.
- Update test comments that attributed the Android 11+ requirement for
  web PiP tests to PictureInPicture#isEnabled.

Original change's description:
> [PiP] Unify PiP eligibility checks on Android
>
> PictureInPicture#isEnabled and
> FullscreenVideoPictureInPictureController#canDoPictureInPicture
> previously performed overlapping eligibility checks separately:
> PictureInPicture#isEnabled checked the Android SDK version,
> FEATURE_PICTURE_IN_PICTURE, and
> AppOpsManager.OPSTR_PICTURE_IN_PICTURE,
> while FullscreenVideoPictureInPictureController checked
> FEATURE_PICTURE_IN_PICTURE and BrowserUiUtils#isAndroidAutoProjected
> inline without checking AppOpsManager.
>
> Move the BrowserUiUtils#isAndroidAutoProjected check into
> PictureInPicture#isEnabled so all device, display, and system-setting
> PiP checks share a single source of truth:
> - Update
>   ActivityTabWebContentsDelegateAndroid#isPictureInPictureEnabled
>   to pass mActivity directly to PictureInPicture#isEnabled.
> - Update
>   FullscreenVideoPictureInPictureController#canDoPictureInPicture
>   to delegate to PictureInPicture#isEnabled(mActivity).
>
> Bug: 568761242

Use DCHECK for extension ID validation in ExtensionSettingsPolicyHandler

In production, policies can contain invalid or unexpected keys, which
causes the CHECK macro in SanitizePolicySettings to fail and crash the
process.

This change reverts the CHECK back to a DCHECK with a TODO comment to
prevent crashes in release builds while maintaining debug assertions.

This fix was generated by the Crash Autofix pipeline for crash report
crash/f2fe862970400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

6df6650681105ecfc786decb995fc5e0fd6a48e4
parent
e0989f500a7903768baed0218c2f6472654a9acb [ diff ] 
Move //c/b/ui/views/* interactive UI tests to per-directory targets

Move these tests out of //chrome/test:interactive_ui_tests into
:interactive_ui_tests targets in their own directories.

Remove the leaf deps that only these tests needed from chrome/test.
footer_interactive_uitest.cc now builds with -Wexit-time-destructors,
so its static DeepQuery becomes a function. The compose and location_bar
targets get no_exit_time_destructors, like chrome/test, because their
tests have many static DeepQuery constants.

This is part of an effort to shrink chrome/test/BUILD.gn by moving test
sources into per-directory targets.

Verification:
- gn check passes on Linux (debug and release), ChromeOS, ChromeOS
  device (betty), Windows (x64 and x86), Mac and Android.
- gn desc runtime_deps for interactive_ui_tests is identical before and
  after on Linux (debug and release), ChromeOS, Windows (x64 and x86)
  and Mac.
- interactive_ui_tests links on Linux (debug and release), ChromeOS,
  Windows (x64 and x86) and Mac.
- interactive_ui_tests test lists are identical before and after:
    Linux:    5647 -> 5647
    ChromeOS: 5510 -> 5510

      compared test lists on Linux and ChromeOS

Ash: Migrate file change notifications off ProfileHelper

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to decouple ChromeOS
code from the main Chrome browser codebase. By migrating away from
ash::ProfileHelper (//chrome/browser/ash) to lower-level Ash APIs such
as ash::BrowserContextHelper (//chromeos/ash/components), it removes
inappropriate upward dependencies from lower-level Ash components to
the higher-level browser application layer. This modernization also
moves code away from heavy, UI-bound Profile objects and toward the
cleaner content::BrowserContext abstraction. Where ProfileHelper wraps
user checks, the migration uses components/user_manager directly.

FileSystemBackend and ObservableFileSystemOperationImpl now use the
browser-context mapping directly when routing file-change notifications
to the correct user. This keeps the account-to-context round trip below
the Chrome profile layer and removes the directory's final ProfileHelper
dependency.

Ash: Migrate AutocorrectManager off ProfileHelper

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to decouple ChromeOS
code from the main Chrome browser codebase. By migrating away from
ash::ProfileHelper (//chrome/browser/ash) to lower-level Ash APIs such
as ash::BrowserContextHelper (//chromeos/ash/components), it removes
inappropriate upward dependencies from lower-level Ash components to
the higher-level browser application layer. This modernization also
moves code away from heavy, UI-bound Profile objects and toward the
cleaner content::BrowserContext abstraction. Where ProfileHelper wraps
user checks, the migration uses components/user_manager directly.

AutocorrectManager now calls ash::IsUserBrowserContext directly when
enabling physical-keyboard autocorrect by default. This is the exact
predicate wrapped by ProfileHelper::IsUserProfile, so all later profile
and session guards retain their existing order and behavior.

Ash: Migrate Flex attestation off ProfileHelper

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to decouple ChromeOS
code from the main Chrome browser codebase. By migrating away from
ash::ProfileHelper (//chrome/browser/ash) to lower-level Ash APIs such
as ash::BrowserContextHelper (//chromeos/ash/components), it removes
inappropriate upward dependencies from lower-level Ash components to
the higher-level browser application layer. This modernization also
moves code away from heavy, UI-bound Profile objects and toward the
cleaner content::BrowserContext abstraction. Where ProfileHelper wraps
user checks, the migration uses components/user_manager directly.

FlexAttester now resolves the signed-in user through the session's
browser-context mapping. The tests annotate each profile with the
account it represents, providing the direct association required by
the lower-level lookup instead of relying on the old primary-user
fallback.

[ log ]
author

Thu Oct 08 05:13:24 2026
tree
8684df34062dbc063835ffceb25e74bd3e141d82
parent
0e9b40199a844829d8a5d2c0277cee52e4ae618f [ diff ] 
[sql] Remove migration TODOs from ColumnTime() and ColumnTimeDelta()

The comments on sql::Statement::ColumnTime() and ColumnTimeDelta() list
the hand-written conversions that they replace, with TODOs to migrate
all callers and then remove the list.

crrev.com/c/7455767 moved the single-line BindInt64() writes to
BindTime() and BindTimeDelta(), and crrev.com/c/8382856, 8410045,
8474691, 8479611, 8505857 and 8521677 moved the remaining reads and
writes of time columns. Outside of tests, no sql::Statement reads a
time column through FromInternalValue() or
FromDeltaSinceWindowsEpoch(Microseconds()) any more, so remove the list
and the TODOs.

Instead, describe what ColumnTime() and ColumnTimeDelta() do and which
hand-written conversions they are preferred over, in the same form as
the BindTime() and BindTimeDelta() comments.

A few places still convert by hand, for reasons these accessors don't
cover:

- PaymentsAutofillTable stores the offer expiry in milliseconds.
- HistoryDatabase and the IndexedDB SQLite backend keep timestamps in
  sql::MetaTable, and BtmDatabase keeps one in its own config table.
  These are generic int64 key-value stores.
- The record_replay TaskDatabase stores times from protobuf int64
  fields, so it binds its pruning cutoff as an int64 too.
- VisitDatabase binds an open-ended end time as the int64_t maximum,
  which is a query bound with a sentinel value.

R=grt@chromium.org

Ash: Migrate DebugDaemonLogSource off ProfileHelper

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to decouple ChromeOS
code from the main Chrome browser codebase. By migrating away from
ash::ProfileHelper (//chrome/browser/ash) to lower-level Ash APIs such
as ash::BrowserContextHelper (//chromeos/ash/components), it removes
inappropriate upward dependencies from lower-level Ash components to
the higher-level browser application layer. This modernization also
moves code away from heavy, UI-bound Profile objects and toward the
cleaner content::BrowserContext abstraction. Where ProfileHelper wraps
user checks, the migration uses components/user_manager directly.

DebugDaemonLogSource now asks BrowserContextHelper directly for each
logged-in user's browser-context path. This preserves the existing
path lookup while removing the ProfileHelper wrapper dependency from
the source.

Ash: Migrate ambient profile lookup off ProfileHelper

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to decouple ChromeOS
code from the main Chrome browser codebase. By migrating away from
ash::ProfileHelper (//chrome/browser/ash) to lower-level Ash APIs such
as ash::BrowserContextHelper (//chromeos/ash/components), it removes
inappropriate upward dependencies from lower-level Ash components to

user checks, the migration uses components/user_manager directly.

AmbientClientImpl and ManagedScreensaverBrowserTest now resolve user
profiles through the shared Ash browser-context mapping. This keeps
ambient mode and its policy coverage on the same lower-level boundary
and removes the directory's remaining dependency on ProfileHelper.

9dc63333f297da621482dc4b24244dccfdf3518a [ diff ] 
[FSA] Validate paths in DeserializeHandle

Previously, FileSystemAccessManagerImpl::DeserializeHandle constructed
file system URLs from serialized handle protobufs without validating
root or relative path invariants. Non-relative child paths, parent
directory references, current-directory segments, or invalid root paths
could resolve outside the root permission path, alias paths in lock
management, or fail URL cracking invariants. Additionally, an unset
protobuf data case triggered a NOTREACHED crash.

Validate root and relative paths in DeserializeHandle before creating
transfer tokens. Reject empty or parent-referencing root paths, require
local root paths to be absolute or Android content URIs, and reject
relative paths containing parent references, current-directory segments,
root separators, or drive-letter prefixes. Verify that non-empty
relative paths resolve underneath the root URL, and drop the transfer
token when the protobuf data case is unset.

For sandboxed handles, reject non-plain relative paths (including parent
references and current-directory segments) and invalid URLs.
ObfuscatedFileUtil strips '.' via VirtualPath::GetComponents(), whereas
FileSystemAccessLockManager preserves '.' via FilePath::GetComponents(),
so allowing '.' segments would alias paths without sharing locks.

Ash: Migrate portal sign-in test off RunUntilIdle

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to modernize Ash tests by
migrating them away from RunUntilIdle() and toward targeted
synchronization with APIs such as RunUntil(), TestFuture, RunLoop
callbacks, observer waiters, or exact mock-time advancement.

NetworkPortalSigninControllerBrowserTest checks that portal sign-in
uses a separate profile. The browser and its profile are available when
the sign-in call returns, so check them directly without draining tasks.
The page does not need to finish loading for this profile check.

BackgroundModeManager::BackgroundModeData::OnProfileWillBeDestroyed.

crash/95cf5fa808400000. Please see go/crash-autofix for more details.

[histogram-cleanup] Remove expired Extensions.HostedAppLaunchContainer

This removes the Extensions.HostedAppLaunchContainer histogram, which
expired on 2023-04-16 and was never extended. It recorded which
container a hosted app was launched in, and nobody is looking at that
data anymore, so the recording code is just dead weight. To clean it up
I deleted the UMA_HISTOGRAM_ENUMERATION call in the app launch code,
dropped the histogram_macros.h include that only existed for it, and
removed the histogram entry from the extensions histograms.xml. The
AppLaunchContainer enum stays since WebApp.LaunchContainer still uses
it.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[Extensions.HostedAppLaunchContainer]=expired

This change was generated using the skill histogram-cleanup.

parent
37054a3ffa91e17f7f9780be1e660f49cfad69a2 [ diff ] 
[Vertical Tabs] Feed the rail hover controller from the rail drag listener

The rail hover controller gets drag events two ways:
VerticalTabRailLayout#dispatchDragEvent for most actions, and each drag
delegate's handleDragExit() for ACTION_DRAG_EXITED, which is never
dispatched. Every new delegate must remember the second path.

Wrap the rail container's drag listener so it reports each event to the
hover controller before TabSwitcherDragHandler. EXITED joins the
controller's switch, and the delegate calls and the RailEventListener
drag hook are removed. No behavior change: the container is the rail's
only drag listener, so it receives the same events plus EXITED.

682054e02594c28f1ce86fb413f0ca545887c198
parent
27fbfeac0b69eddfa01c55b15ce858fea0afdf5e [ diff ] 
[Vertical Tabs] Register the rail drag listener once on the rail container

With listeners on each rail view, ACTION_DRAG_EXITED fires whenever the
pointer crosses between views, not only when it leaves the rail.

Register the shared drag handler only on the rail container, which
covers the header, both lists, the new tab button and the margins, and
remove the per-view listeners. A droppable descendant would win over the
container in ViewGroup#findFrontmostDroppableChildAt, so no rail
descendant may register its own listener.

New behavior: a hover-expanded rail collapses when a drag leaves it, via
the delegates' handleDragExit(). It does not collapse at drag end: a
drag that ends outside the rail has already exited it, and a drop or ESC
over the rail keeps it expanded under the pointer.

The decor-view relay still delivers ACTION_DRAG_ENDED when the rail
detaches.

MiraclePtr: Introduce UnprotectedInReleaseForPerformance trait

Add UnprotectedInReleaseForPerformance to RawPtrTraits in PartitionAlloc
for pointers identified as verified performance hotspots (e.g. in
Speedometer 3).

Also add the remove_unprotected_in_release_trait GN argument and
REMOVE_UNPROTECTED_IN_RELEASE_TRAIT buildflag (default false), allowing
protection on UnprotectedInRelease to subsequently be enabled without
affecting performance hotspots or debug/DCHECK builds.

Annotate verified performance hotspot fields across Blink HTML
tokenizer/parser, graphics/font metrics, and base task runner/message
loop with UnprotectedInReleaseForPerformance.
Plan: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1QnD9RuBDKb7j4f0eM7izoaYU7yx4vg5WBXugLCHWcM8/edit?usp=sharing

b2c4f2bef84dd03948f7e3a930043fd2e421556d
parent
274a2d2cc202a0ebe58e2e21ee14e33ddf0f86a6 [ diff ] 
[SafeBrowsing][iOS] Increase CSD verdict caching timeout in EG test

SafeBrowsingTestCase/testClientSideDetectionRuns verifies that client-side
detection (CSD) completes and caches a classification verdict.
Under bot contention on iOS simulators, cold-start model loading, page
snapshotting, and BEST_EFFORT background ML feature extraction frequently
take 40-55 seconds on initial execution runs, exceeding the 30-second
kClassificationVerdictTimeout.

This caused a near-100% initial failure rate on the ios-simulator CI
builder (passing only on retry), and exhausted retries when bot load
spiked (e.g. build 156243 on iPhone 16 27.0).

Increase kClassificationVerdictTimeout from 30s to 60s to ensure
adequate margin on loaded simulator bots and prevent flakiness.

Bug: None

Trim //chrome/test:browser_tests leaf deps (3/3)

Most browser_tests sources have moved into per-directory source_sets,
but the deps they needed stayed behind in chrome/test/BUILD.gn. Remove
leaf deps that the remaining sources no longer include. This is part 3
of 3; the parts touch different regions of the browser_tests target and
can land in any order.

Kept: per-directory test aggregators and other test targets, test
support targets, and non-C++ targets such as resources, strings,
generated headers and Windows .rc resources, since gn check doesn't
cover them.

This is part of an effort to shrink chrome/test/BUILD.gn by moving test
sources into per-directory targets.

Verification, for this CL alone and for all 3 parts together:
- gn check passes on Linux (debug and release), ChromeOS, ChromeOS
  device (betty), Windows (x64 and x86), Mac, Android desktop and
  Android phone.
- gn desc runtime_deps, defines, include_dirs and cflags for
  browser_tests are identical before and after on Linux (debug and
  release), ChromeOS, Windows (x64 and x86) and Mac.

  (x64 and x86) and Mac.

13bf035a15044c3fd8fb04cbec3ae4b86bd5d1d8 [ diff ] 
[BottomSheet] Relayout container when layout change updates sheet

This CL re-measures and lays out the bottom sheet container when a
container layout change updates the sheet dimensions. During device
rotation, the sheet width is updated inside the container layout
listener after the container has already laid out its children, leaving
the sheet at its previous size. Laying out the container again applies
the updated dimensions in the same layout pass.

Before (portrait): http://screen/8wf3E2xJV6PAkzK
After (portrait): http://screen/8VA89WUBL2P3sF7
Before (landscape): http://screen/7oiYTucfyXyqqGj
After (landscape): http://screen/6pWgn8T7tnQkJzQ

Decisions API: Require user activation to download the model

DecisionModel.create() only rejected an unavailable model, so once a
backend reports "downloadable", any page could start a multi-GB model
download without a user gesture. Like LanguageModel, SemanticEmbedder
and the other built-in AI APIs, create() now rejects with
NotAllowedError when availability is "downloadable" and the window has
no user activation (sticky activation is enough, as for the other
APIs). Workers skip the check, as they do for the other APIs.

Adds a WPT like the other *-create-user-activation tests.

Decisions API: Add shared schema compiler and flag

Setup the backend-agnostic browser core for the Built-in AI Decisions
API (`window.DecisionModel`) shared by the EmbeddingGemma and Gemma 4
backends:

- `DecisionModelSchemaCompiler`:
  - Validates `context` and `questions` (`boolean`, `choice`, `score`),
    matching Blink's schema constraints.
  - Normalizes per-option weights into `AIDecisionModelResult` with top
    label, confidence, probability distribution, and expected `score`
    (`DecodeResult`).
- `AIManager`:
  - Adds `GetLanguages` / `CheckAndFixOutputLanguage` specializations
    and `CheckDecisionModelOptions` to validate language and schema
    options in `CanCreateDecisionModel` and `CreateDecisionModel`.
- Adds `chrome://flags#decisions-api` and unit tests
  (`DecisionModelSchemaCompilerTest` and `AIManagerTest.CanCreateDecisionModel`).

285434149076058b62b2725d4fbbf14bac3eed25 [ diff ] 
chrome/browser: decouple WebUI resource .harddeps from core and UI C++ targets

Currently, several C++ targets in //chrome/browser pull in order-only
dependencies (.harddeps) on WebUI TypeScript/Rollup/Grit resource
generation steps (such as //chrome/browser/resources/glic:resources and
//chrome/browser/resources/settings:resources), forcing thousands of
unrelated C++ compilation units across //chrome/browser:core,
//chrome/browser/ui, //chrome/browser/ui/tabs:impl,
//chrome/browser/devtools, and //chrome/browser/extensions to wait for
WebUI bundling and grit steps before compiling:

1. Remove redundant/unneeded "//chrome:extra_resources" dependencies
   from //chrome/browser/devtools, //chrome/browser/extensions, and
   //chrome/browser/ui/webui/private_ai_internals.
2. Set check_includes_strict = true on //chrome/browser/ui/webui:configs
   and //chrome/browser/ui/webui:chrome_web_ui_controller_factory so
   that private dependencies on individual WebUI targets do not leak
   their .harddeps through those two registration headers.
3. Move 4 headers included by //chrome/browser:core from
   //chrome/browser/glic:impl to //chrome/browser/glic so that
   //chrome/browser:core and //chrome/browser/ui/webui:configs do not
   need to depend on //chrome/browser/glic:impl (which depends on
   //chrome/browser/resources/glic:resources).

565eb5895fd1d36fb39250fe129fe0e7fc3cc51c [ diff ] 
[OpenScreen] Fix media perf sender install and tunnel failures

Follow-up to the Sender refactor. These are behavior fixes for failures
that were silently ignored or reported misleadingly on the perf bots:

- Windows: the C:/cft_temp mkdir command was a plain string with
  doubled braces and a literal "{tmp}", so it never ran as intended.
  It is now a real f-string and is checked.
- POSIX install: curl uses -f so HTTP errors fail instead of saving an
  error page as the zip. Download/unzip, xattr, and chmod are checked,
  and the install is re-verified afterwards.
- Windows install: curl.exe uses -f. Because PowerShell does not
  propagate native exit codes, the install is re-verified with the
  EXISTS check. Writing the launch script and schtasks /create and /run
  are checked. /delete stays unchecked since the task may not exist.
- ChromeOS: _install_chromedriver no longer swallows install errors and
  reuses Sender.driver_path instead of a second CfT lookup.
- SSH tunnel: uses the shared SSH_BASE_OPTS (BatchMode, ConnectTimeout)
  and fails fast with SenderSshError if ssh exits during startup, e.g.
  when a stale tunnel holds the port (ExitOnForwardFailure).
- Local installs pick the Chrome binary from the sender OS, not the
  host's sys.platform.
- binary_location is set from Sender.chrome_binary_path() in all three
  scripts. Behavior change: Linux senders in the cast and videostack
  tests now launch the installed Chrome for Testing that matches
  chromedriver, rather than whatever Chrome is on PATH.
- Monitoring: the Windows CSV is removed with Remove-Item instead of
  rm, and a monitor that ignores terminate() is killed after 5s.
- Bare "except: pass" blocks now log.

os_version is still only logged for diagnostics.

The Windows paths are covered by unit tests but could not be exercised
on real hardware.

[WebMCP] Fix crash for documents without a frame

- createHTMLDocument() creates a document without a frame that shares
  its creator's execution context. Reading modelContext on both documents
  binds the same browser host twice and terminates the renderer.
- Return from the ModelContext constructor before binding browser
  interfaces when the document has no frame. Preserve the per-document
  cached object and the existing InvalidStateError method rejections.
- Add regression coverage for both getter orders, active-document tool
  registration, discovery and execution, and inactive-document errors.

Reproducible steps:
- Open dev tools console and type the following
- ```
document.modelContext;
document.implementation.createHTMLDocument().modelContext;
```
- Chromium renderer will crash

[ios blink] Use a noncharacter in the IFT last-font shaping test

ShapesWithLastFontEvenWhenExtensionFails assumes U+0378 has no glyph in system fonts. On iOS, system fallback supplies a glyph, preventing the test from reaching the final fallback to the primary font.
Replace U+0378 with U+FDD0, a noncharacter for which Blink explicitly skips platform font fallback. Preserve the assertions verifying that the primary font renders .notdef even when extension fails.

Revert "Do not fast-shutdown a process that has a frame pending deletion."

This reverts commit 00e0c06b888b9ce2315e4510520df49e5502c7fd.

Reason for revert: Causes deterministic failure of
All/RenderProcessHostTest.NoReentrantCleanupOnSubframeDetachWithWorkerRef/*
on linux-chromeos-chrome

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8668561312793548033

Original change's description:
> Do not fast-shutdown a process that has a frame pending deletion.
>
> RenderProcessHostImpl::Cleanup() calls FastShutdown() when
> HasOnlyNonLiveRenderFrameHosts() returns true. That function ignored
> RenderFrameHosts that are in the pending deletion state.
>
> A subframe can detach while its process is crashed. During the detach,
> CancelNavigation() can release the last refcount on the process.
> Cleanup() then calls FastShutdown(), which deletes the FrameTreeNode.
> CancelNavigation() then uses the deleted FrameTreeNode.
>
> To fix this, HasOnlyNonLiveRenderFrameHosts() now returns false if a
> RenderFrameHost is pending deletion. Cleanup() stays synchronous. The
> process exits when the frame finishes deletion through the normal
> RemoveRoute() -> Cleanup() path.
>
> This CL also fixes some stale linter paths that presubmit checks were
> complaining about.
>
> Bug: 569408565
> Change-Id: I2a8361143a7d9e46c1d9727e00f050ae4bdd6ecd
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8519561
> Reviewed-by: Alex Moshchuk <alexmos@chromium.org>
> Commit-Queue: Liam Brady <lbrady@google.com>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1713828}

a015cf81f0671ef4625427ec0551b9d360f3b4a1 [ diff ] 
Trim //chrome/test:browser_tests leaf deps (2/3)

Most browser_tests sources have moved into per-directory source_sets,
but the deps they needed stayed behind in chrome/test/BUILD.gn. Remove
leaf deps that the remaining sources no longer include. This is part 2
of 3; the parts touch different regions of the browser_tests target and
can land in any order.

Kept: per-directory test aggregators and other test targets, test
support targets, and non-C++ targets such as resources, strings,
generated headers and Windows .rc resources, since gn check doesn't
cover them.

This is part of an effort to shrink chrome/test/BUILD.gn by moving test
sources into per-directory targets.

Verification, for this CL alone and for all 3 parts together:
- gn check passes on Linux (debug and release), ChromeOS, ChromeOS
  device (betty), Windows (x64 and x86), Mac, Android desktop and
  Android phone.
- gn desc runtime_deps, defines, include_dirs and cflags for

[test] Fix dangling RunLoop in DataSharing SDK test

DataSharingSDKDelegateDesktopBrowserTest.ReadGroupLoadsWebContents
passes a callback that quits a stack RunLoop, but never runs the loop.
The test body returns before the reply arrives, so a late reply calls
Quit() on a destroyed RunLoop and the test crashes in
base::RunLoop::Quit. This already happens in about 1 of 5 runs on
linux-chromeos-dbg, and in every run when WebUIReloadButtonStudy is
enabled on ChromeOS, which changes startup timing.

The test only checks that ReadGroupDeprecated creates the SDK
WebContents, so pass base::DoNothing() as the callback.

  --gtest_filter=DataSharingSDKDelegateDesktopBrowserTest.*

248dbdaf0dc87a39f649c55c4baa970fde54692c [ diff ] 
base: Check ThreadPool worker creation succeeds

Check that WorkerThread::Start() obtained a valid thread handle instead
of returning false. Pooled single-thread task runners ignore this return
value and can accept tasks for a worker that will never execute them.

Use NotFatalUntil::M160 to collect non-fatal reports in official builds
before making thread creation failure fatal in M160, like memory
allocation failure. This allows evaluating the failure rate before
enabling process termination.

tree
ef2a5fdd59d031d5122843c4452c6516dcbf844f
parent
ccbc6cacb39a9849d67be95fba5efd7a68c7fc39 [ diff ] 
[icu] Handle infinite Time values in TimeZone::GetOffset/InDaylightTime

crrev.com/c/8483536 switches base::i18n::TimeZone::GMT() from
icu::OlsonTimeZone("GMT") (loaded from the zoneinfo64 resource bundle)
to icu::SimpleTimeZone (cloned from icu::TimeZone::getGMT()).

Without this CL, that switch would introduce a DCHECK failure when
calling TimeZone::GMT().GetOffset() (or the 4-argument
TimeFormatAsIso8601() / TimeFormatAsIso8601WithTimeZone() with
TimeZone::GMT()) on Time::Min() or Time::Max():
- Time::Min() and Time::Max() return -infinity and +infinity from
  InMillisecondsFSinceUnixEpoch().
- icu::OlsonTimeZone("GMT") has no finalZone and returns 0 from
  getOffset() without inspecting the UDate argument.
- icu::SimpleTimeZone::getOffset() converts the UDate argument to
  calendar fields via Grego::timeToFields(), which fails with
  U_ILLEGAL_ARGUMENT_ERROR on non-finite values and trips
  DCHECK(U_SUCCESS(status)) in TimeZone::GetOffset().

We land this CL before crrev.com/c/8483536 to prevent this regression
by handling time.is_inf() explicitly in TimeZone::GetOffset() and
TimeZone::InDaylightTime(). At the same time, this fixes pre-existing
DCHECK(U_SUCCESS(status)) failures on Time::Min() and Time::Max() for:
- TimeZone::GetOffset() on SimpleTimeZone instances (such as
  TimeZone::Unknown()) and on OlsonTimeZone instances with a finalZone
  (such as "America/Los_Angeles" on Time::Max()).
- TimeZone::InDaylightTime() on all timezones (since
  icu::TimeZone::inDaylightTime() always converts UDate via
  GregorianCalendar).

7f17d069d7150dba3498893323047c871d101378 [ diff ] 
Fix http/tests/security/webgl-remote-read-remote-image-*.html

These tests were marked [ Timeout ] on Mac14-arm64 and Mac15-arm64 when
those bots were brought up (crrev.com/c/5332802, crrev.com/c/5750526).
At that time SwiftShader WebGL contexts were refused on arm64 Macs
(crbug.com/1378476), so getContext("webgl") returned null and
gl.createTexture() threw an uncaught TypeError before notifyDone() could
run. crrev.com/c/5675974 enabled --enable-unsafe-swiftshader for web
tests and removed the Mac11/12/13-arm64 NeverFixTests entries, but
missed these lines; the tests have passed on Mac14/15-arm64 since then.

Remove the stale expectations (including the adjacent
http/tests/canvas/webgl/origin-clean-conformance.html entries from the
same CLs), and harden the tests so a missing WebGL context or resource
load error logs FAIL and calls notifyDone() instead of timing out.

Bug: None

ad24db7643e3b3ddfac67fccee3f4daa04305ccf [ diff ] 
[glanceables] Remove four expired Ash.Glanceables.Api.Tasks histograms

Ash.Glanceables.Api.Tasks.{ProcessedTasksCount,RawTasksCount,
SimultaneousMarkAsCompletedRequestsCount,TaskListsCount} expired on
2025-12-31, so the code that records them is dead.
tools/metrics/histograms/README.md: "Once a histogram has expired, the
code that records it becomes dead code and should be removed from the
codebase."

Remove the four recording calls in TasksClientImpl, the test assertions
that only checked these histograms and the histograms.xml entries. The
Ash.Glanceables.Api.{Method}.{Latency,PagesCount,Status} histograms
recorded by the same class are defined separately and are left alone.
No behavior change.

R=achuith@chromium.org

Bug: None

Refactor EcheAppManagerFactory and LaunchedAppInfo

- Simplify LaunchedAppInfo into an aggregate struct and initialize it
  using designated initializers.
- Remove Profile* dependency from anonymous namespace helpers in
  eche_app_manager_factory.cc by passing EcheAppManager* directly.
- Remove redundant EnsureStreamClose, StreamGoBack, and BubbleShown
  wrapper functions and bind EcheAppManager methods directly (or
  base::DoNothing() when EcheAppManager is null in tests).
- Simplify URL construction in LaunchWebApp using base::StrCat and
  base::StrAppend.

[Organizer] Fix focusing logic

This fixes several issues with the focusing logic:
 - The search box is now focused when the organizer panel is shown
 - Focus is correctly returned to the previous view when the panel is
   hidden, even when in the VTS; this prevents a state where nothing is
   focused and keyboard navigation isn't possible.

Detailed tests to be added in a follow-up.

bfefdd45c6a1fedd16deefdfceea28a3af6e48ba [ diff ] 
[Ambient] Show account pictures for federated credentials

Adds an optional account picture to ambient_signin::FederatedCredential.
When set, the ambient login bubble shows it instead of the default icon,
both in the collapsed view and in the expanded credential list.

The picture must come from the identity provider, never from the
requesting site. The caller crops it so that it is square, and the
bubble scales it to the standard 20x20 icon size. FedCM will pass the
account picture, cropped into a circle like in its own UI, in a
follow-up CL.

Also adds tests that the bubble keeps its fixed width of 320 DIPs, which
PermissionPromptBubbleBaseView sets, and elides long text such as long
account names, emails or provider names, while the buttons keep their
size. This covers both the collapsed view and the expanded credential
list. There is no behavior change for this part.

[MiraclePtr] Enforce raw_ptr in templated containers for user_script_loader_unittest.cc

This change replaces instances of raw C-style pointers (`T*`) with
`raw_ptr<T>` for elements stored inside templated containers (such as
`std::vector` and `std::array`). This aligns with the broader MiraclePtr
safety initiative to protect against use-after-free vulnerabilities.

This advances MiraclePtr's expansion to containers and preempts an
upcoming clang-plugin update that will strictly ban these un-rewritten
instances.

rewriting to `raw_ptr` was performed.

Bug: b:501142609
Doc: go/miracleptr-in-containers

parent
3ab9f85874d939e29f1179d7184e79f3cdc58a47 [ diff ] 
[SessionRestore] Notify observers when profile session restore ends

Previously, SessionRestore notified observers when all initial tabs
finished loading across windows, but did not notify when an individual
profile's restore completed nor report whether any session restore
remained in flight.

This adds SessionRestoreObserver::OnProfileSessionRestored() and
SessionRestore::IsAnySessionCurrentlyRestoring() to track the
completion of session restores per profile.

To support this:
- SessionRestoreObserver gains OnProfileSessionRestored() to report
  the number of restored normal and app windows for a profile.
- SessionRestoreImpl leaves the active restorer set before notifying
  observers in its destructor, so that IsAnySessionCurrentlyRestoring()
  reflects completion during the notification callback.
- SessionRestore tracks foreign session restores at construction and
  excludes them from IsAnySessionCurrentlyRestoring() and observer
  notifications.

This is part 1 of 3 in a chain adding the
SessionRestore.ProfileDiffAfterRestart UMA:
1. crrev.com/c/8515990: Notify observers when profile session restore
   ends (this CL)
2. crrev.com/c/8518625: Add ProfileDiffAfterRestartRecorder
3. crrev.com/c/8392863: Integrate ProfileDiffAfterRestartRecorder into
   startup

Coauthored using Antigravity.

Bug: 554057178
Test: browser_tests --gtest_filter='SessionRestoreTest.OnProfileSessionRestored*:SessionRestoreTest.ForeignRestoresDoNotNotifyObservers'

a81b6dbc00bbb4e1244d26f6887ea96a20a435e3
parent
776f08979793238c681bed5ab4e4a0fe3ffc6b0e [ diff ] 
omnibox: Create the WebUI popup widget on first show

Every browser window creates its WebUI omnibox popups (classic or full,
plus AIM) up front, and each presenter also built a native widget for
its popup at that time. A hidden popup needs no native window, and on
ChromeOS each extra widget adds work to browser window creation.

The presenter now leaves its WebUI content in the owned container and
creates the widget on the first Show(); the WebContents is still
created and loaded with the window, so the renderer warm-up is kept.
The rest of the presenter already handles a missing widget.

With this change no popup widget exists until a popup is shown, down
from two per window. The resource budget test now caps widgets at one
across all windows, which covers the full popup shown on startup.

This is guarded by kOmniboxWebUIPopupCreateWidgetOnShow (enabled by
default) as a kill switch; it should be added to the ChromeOS perf
flags Tast fixture.

TAG=agy
CONV=a03590ac-da78-4264-b0b6-3ac7536d2e06

[Direct Offers] Match offers on the merchant's registrable domain

Offers now apply to any URL with the same eTLD+1 as one of the offer's
merchant origins, matching how loyalty cards are matched (see
LoyaltyCard::GetAffiliationCategory()). For example, an offer for
https://store.example.com/ applies to https://example.com/ and
https://www.example.com/, but not to https://notexample.com/.

📋 Metadata

74f2e430090110693f78f1991f5e40ee12e9d473

tree
12c9470f266d6670e1b047b562826168da761564
parent
d5e9e97a9927ead09c7027663204b5df9fa59b37 [ diff ] 
Add metric to record item selection when Fusebox attachments popup closes

Tracks whether a user selected at least one item (such as an attachment
button, picker, or model option) during a single presentation of the
Fusebox attachments popup. The boolean histogram
Omnibox.MobileFusebox.AttachmentsPopupItemSelected is recorded whenever
the popup closes, regardless of whether it was dismissed via an item
selection, a back press, tapping the plus button, or unfocusing the
omnibox.

parent
08a9ebf16fd851b58cafb35f0c8b03570fc9be36 [ diff ] 
Fall back to the native UI when the WebView2 UI cannot be created

With --webviewui, a WebView2 creation failure was previously ignored
and the install ran with a blank window. In the current
gfx::WindowImpl-based window, the COM STA check in OnCreate returned
-1, which WindowImpl::Init treats as fatal via NOTREACHED(). A
synchronous CreateCoreWebView2EnvironmentWithOptions failure, such as
a missing WebView2 runtime, was reported through a SequencedTaskRunner
that never runs while the UI thread pumps Win32 messages.

WebView2ProgressWnd::Initialize() now checks the COM apartment, the
WebView2 runtime, and the user data directory before any window
exists, and returns an HRESULT so the controller can pick the native
UI up front. WebView2UI::Create() returns synchronous failures to the
caller. Any failure after the window is shown runs a creation-failed
callback: AppInstallControllerImpl shows the native ProgressWnd while
the WebView2 window is still visible, so the native window can take
the foreground, and the WebView2 window then hides and closes itself
without quitting the UI message loop. If the native window can't be
created either, the install proceeds silently through
InstallProgressSilentObserver, which ends the UI message loop on
completion.

Progress notifications reach the UI through a new
AppInstallProgressForwarder, owned by the controller on the UI thread.
AppInstallProgressIPC targets the forwarder, whose address is stable,
so the UI can be replaced without touching the IPC adapter. The
forwarder replays the most recent notification to a new target, so a
window that takes over after the install completed, for example after
a fast startup error via Exit(), shows the completion state instead of
staying in its initial state. The main sequence is told about the new
window handle and reloads the app logo for it if the install already
started.

Tests: AppInstallProgressForwarderTest covers forwarding, replay of
completion and of progress, and the null target. WebView2UITest covers
Create() returning synchronous failures. WebView2ProgressWndTest covers
Initialize() failing without a runtime. The runtime-missing cases point
the WebView2 loader at an empty folder via
WEBVIEW2_BROWSER_EXECUTABLE_FOLDER.

Tested manually on Windows 11 by pointing the WebView2 loader override
(HKLM\SOFTWARE\Policies\Microsoft\Edge\WebView2\BrowserExecutableFolder)
at an empty folder: the native progress UI is shown and the install
completes. With the override removed, the WebView2 UI is shown as
before.

[MiraclePtr] Enforce raw_ptr in templated containers for NativeWidgetMacNSWindowHost

This change replaces instances of raw C-style pointers (`T*`) with
`raw_ptr<T>` for elements stored inside templated containers (such as
`std::vector` and `std::array`). This aligns with the broader MiraclePtr
safety initiative to protect against use-after-free vulnerabilities.

This advances MiraclePtr's expansion to containers and preempts an
upcoming clang-plugin update that will strictly ban these un-rewritten
instances.

Bug: b:501142609
Doc: go/miracleptr-in-containers
Max-Compile-Failures: 100

f4bac2d545950fdc9b10d897c5a42c1572093edd [ diff ] 
Refactor PrefetchManagerTest.Throttles to ExpectationHandler

Replaces `ControllableHttpResponse` with `ExpectationHandler` in
`PrefetchManagerTest.Throttles`.

The test uses fixed static responses and does not require dynamic,
delayed, or request-dependent response handling. Refactoring to
`ExpectationHandler` simplifies the test setup and improves readability
while preserving existing behavior and coverage.

Sync separate GL texture content on write access in ExternalVkImageBacking
📋 Metadata
commit
7f1931770c353b2770af4c8bdd15e0f20b7dbb01
[ log ]
author
Tom Anderson <thomasanderson@chromium.org>
Thu Oct 08 00:12:04 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Thu Oct 08 00:12:04 2026
tree
312929cba76aff252cb7835c4bf0f3af93986477
parent
5b36ee6d7570f83e656a989429a691790b3196f6 [ diff ] 
Sync separate GL texture content on write access in ExternalVkImageBacking

crrev.com/c/8436737 made ExternalVkImageBacking reject the separate GL
texture mode under WebGPU GL-Vk interop and stopped UpdateContent() from
masking failed copies. This handles the remaining issues:

- The interop/separate-GL bail-outs in Create() and CreateFromGMB() ran
  after the VkImage(s) had been allocated/imported. Returning nullptr
  there drops VulkanImages without calling Destroy(), which hits
  DCHECK(!device_queue_) and leaks the images in release builds. Move
  the checks ahead of any allocation. Likewise, destroy the images
  already allocated for earlier planes if a later plane fails to
  allocate in Create().

- BeginAccess() only synchronized content between the separate GL
  texture and the VkImage on read-only access. On write/read-write
  access EndAccess() then marks the written side as exclusively holding
  the latest content, so a partial write or READWRITE access that does
  not overwrite everything exposes stale or uninitialized storage.
  Synchronize the accessed side on write accesses too, after rejecting
  conflicting accesses up front so no copy runs for an access that will
  fail.

- Only report descriptor.isInitialized to Dawn when the VkImage holds
  the latest content, so a failed GL->Vk sync is not treated as valid
  data.

[html-in-canvas] Do not treat canvas content as effectively invisible

This patch resets effective invisibility when painting drawable
elements inside a <canvas content="drawable"> element. Previously, if
the canvas or an ancestor had opacity: 0, ScopedEffectivelyInvisible
marked all descendant paint chunks as effectively invisible.

tree
b91d149c58c3512494b73c0f3f96ef52d42dce13
parent
d31addff83fad5c9843af1120f238a72629dcf1a [ diff ] 
[voice_search][pepc] Ensure height is correct when prompt showing

* In aim_app.css, #composebox.has-permission-prompt::part(composebox) only applied min-height: var(--cr_composebox_minimum_height) and min-width: var(--cr_composebox_minimum_width) to the inner part="composebox" div
* However, this is "display: none" during voice search, so <cr-omnibox-composebox> itself never got the min-height/min-width

* In composebox_voice_search.css , <cr-composebox-voice-search> and
its #container (which renders the glow/shadow box-shadow:
var(--box-shadow-energy-effect)) had a hardcoded
--voice-search-minimum-height: 121px, so even when
.permission-prompt-showing was added, the voice search overlay and its
shadow stayed stuck at 121px tall while the omnibox grew behind it.

The Fix:
* aim_app.css ensure the composebox has the right min height var set
  (temporary fix)
* Add min width/height var in voice search.ts so future users do not
have to hard code min height/width for their composebox container for
future composebox embedders
